rohitg00/agentmemory ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.31 Stable2026-10-26T14:00:39Z

AgentMemory 0.9.31 improves memory retrieval, reduces repeated model work, and fixes capture, recovery and import failures. It also adds native Pi, Oh My Pi and DSH integrations and upgrades the runtime to iii 0.25.0 with Worker Compose.

Retrieval and performance

  • Better keyword ranking: fixing prefix-score inflation raised strict recall-all@5 from 65.74% to 84.68%, a gain of 18.94 percentage points, in the documented BM25 development evaluation. Complete-evidence retrieval increased from 309 to 398 of 470 answerable questions, with 92 recovered cases and 3 regressions. These are component retrieval results on inspected development data, not held-out answer accuracy or a release-wide speed benchmark. Methodology and receipts, #1514.
  • Less repeated summary work: summaries process new observations and merge them into the existing summary. An unchanged session makes zero additional summarization calls and skips repeat reflection, graph extraction and skill processing. #1507.
  • Bounded graph work: search shares indexed graph reads and avoids repeated full-graph enumeration. Optional graph enrichment has a 500 ms budget so ready keyword and vector results can return if graph storage stalls. This is a graph-stage budget, not an overall search-latency guarantee. #1541, #1559.

Fixes

  • Session summaries cover later turns instead of stopping at the first Stop event. Idle sessions finalize, and abandoned sessions are summarized before abandonment. Fixes #745.
  • Saved memories retain project and agent scope and remain recoverable when token budgets shorten or omit content. Recall can target memories, observations or both. Fixes #819 and #1232.
  • Failed compression preserves searchable captures and supports scoped inspection and retry. Vector recovery retains unfinished work across checkpoints, provider failures and restarts. #1546, #1562.
  • Replace imports validate data before deleting stored records. JSONL imports preserve session timestamps and avoid repeated duplicate observations; the first re-import of rows from older versions can still add one final copy. #1576, #1526.
  • Hooks capture final assistant replies and preserve distinct subagent, task and notification events. #1510, #1513.
  • CLI status shows the actual embedding provider, and doctor uses the daemon's LLM-provider detection. Fixes #1488 and #1572.
  • MCP keeps routine logs off stdout and identifies writes that only reached local fallback storage. The viewer loads actions beyond the first page, and deleting memories cleans up their facets. #1522, #1563, #1524, #1591.

Integrations and documentation

Bundled Pi, Oh My Pi and DSH integrations provide native capture and memory workflows. Cursor resolves workspace ownership more reliably, and the Devin plugin captures lifecycle events correctly. Shared agent skills, setup documentation and README translations are updated. The bundled Pi connector works independently of the separate Pi package's npm publication.

Upgrading

Stop AgentMemory with the existing CLI before upgrading, then restart 0.9.31 using the same instance, port and data directory. The runtime now uses iii 0.25.0 with Worker Compose. Viewer updates use authenticated SSE on the REST port; port 3112 is unused. MCP narrative recall returns structured JSON; read its text field.

Re-run agentmemory connect pi --with-mcp or agentmemory connect omp --with-mcp to refresh bundled integrations. Existing DSH hook-bridge installations migrate with agentmemory connect dsh --with-hooks --force. On Windows, WSL2 is recommended; native Windows remains unverified and requires a manually installed pinned engine.

Upgrade guide | 0.9.31 changelog | All changes since 0.9.30

Thanks to our contributors

Thank you @guibeira, @JackieJK, @andrew05060414, @hejiawow, @dmazhukov, @Rex57, @SomSamantray, @yxtay, @akushonkamen, @DimitriChrysafis and @ErikBPF for your contributions to this release, and everyone who reported issues and tested fixes.

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.21CC Switch v4.0.21 Stable2026-10-26T13:47:11Z

CC Switch v4.0.21

本版本为 Claude Code 增加了多个官方订阅账号的切换：在授权中心添加几个 Claude 账号，每张官方卡选一个，切到哪张卡，Claude Code 就用哪个号；想几个号同时用，从卡片上「打开终端」，那个终端固定用卡片上的号。登录走 Claude Code 官方的流程，令牌只留在本机的 Claude Code 里，CC Switch 不上传、不刷新、不登出。绑了账号的卡片和授权中心都会显示每个号的套餐和剩余额度。另外，「打开终端」挪到了卡片上，Codex 的模型列表可以拖动排序，轻量模式重启后保持。修复了托管 ChatGPT 账号重启后误报登录过期、Codex 经路由时请求发到了别的地址、Codex 的 MCP 配置里多出 timeout / autoApprove、npm 12 下 OpenCode 和 Grok Build 装不好、Kimi Code 用量的时间不对、Oh My Pi 的 models.yml 里有 &、*、! 时无法编辑等问题。4.0 完整的更新内容请见 v4.0 发布说明。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

在多个 Claude 官方订阅账号之间切换

手上有不止一个 Claude 订阅（比如个人 Max 和公司 Team，或者多个 Pro 账号）时，以前只能在 Claude Code 里 /logout 再 /login，或者自己给每个号建配置目录。现在可以在 CC Switch 里管理多个号（#1928）。

安全与合规。 订阅账号交给工具管理，最要紧的是令牌安全、不违反 Anthropic 的条款。这个功能按 Claude Code 官方文档的做法实现，令牌始终只在你本机的 Claude Code 里：

  • 登录走官方流程。 添加账号时运行的是 Claude Code 自带的 claude auth login --claudeai，密码和授权都在 claude.ai 的官方页面完成。CC Switch 没有自己的 Claude 登录，也没有自己的 OAuth 客户端。Anthropic 的法律与合规页要求 Claude 账号的登录必须通过 Anthropic 自己的流程完成，并且明确不限制用户用自己的订阅登录未经修改的 Claude Code。
  • 多账号用的是官方推荐的方法。 每个号一个独立的配置目录，用 CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR 区分，和 Claude Code 认证文档里「Log in with multiple accounts」一节的方法一样，CC Switch 只是替你建好目录、打开终端。
  • 令牌不离开本机。 令牌只存在 Claude Code 自己的位置（macOS 的钥匙串，Linux、Windows 的 .credentials.json），不上传，不随云同步，也不写进 CC Switch 的数据库。CC Switch 自己只记邮箱、组织、套餐这类账号资料和登录过期时间，不记令牌。
  • 不替你发请求。 CC Switch 没有服务器，不用订阅令牌调用模型；对话请求都由本机未经修改的 Claude Code 直接发给 Anthropic，用量计在你自己的账号上。查额度时在本机直接查 Anthropic 官方的额度接口，和 Claude Code 自己的 /usage 查的是同一份数据。
  • 不刷新、不登出、不改 Claude Code。 令牌一律由 Claude Code 自己刷新。删除账号只删 CC Switch 为它建的目录（macOS 上连同这个目录在钥匙串里的条目），不会让这个号在任何地方退出登录。CC Switch 不修改 Claude Code 的程序文件。
  • 只管你自己的账号。 不按额度自动轮换账号，也不提供把订阅共享给别人的功能。

切卡即切号会在本机 Claude Code 的存储位置之间移动登录（只按 Claude Code 自己的格式），官方文档没有专门讲到这种用法。只想用文档写明的方式的话，卡片保持「跟随 Claude Code 登录」，其他号只从卡片「打开终端」使用。

怎么用：

  • 添加账号。 在授权中心的 Claude 组点「添加账号」。浏览器会打开 claude.ai 的官方授权页，登录后账号自动出现在列表里。浏览器授权后回不到本机时，可以把地址栏里的地址或页面上的代码粘贴回来。每个号的目录互不干扰，会同步你在 ~/.claude 里的设置、MCP 和 CLAUDE.md（登录凭据和路由相关的设置除外）。
  • 给官方卡选账号。 Claude 官方卡的「登录方式」下拉和 Codex 官方卡一样：上面是已登录的账号，下面是「添加或管理 Claude 账号」（跳到授权中心）和「跟随 Claude Code 登录」。一个号建一张卡就行。
  • 切卡即切号。 切到选了账号的官方卡时，CC Switch 把这个号的登录放进 Claude Code 默认读取的位置，原来那个号放回它自己的目录。终端、IDE 插件、脚本里的 claude 都改用这个号，已经在运行的 Claude Code 也会立即跟着换，不用重启。切到选了账号的官方卡时，现在的登录如果还不在授权中心会先收进来。切换时持有 Claude Code 自己的锁；切换中途 CC Switch 意外退出的话，下次切换或启动时会自动补完或撤回。选「跟随 Claude Code 登录」的卡不碰登录，不添加账号的人不受影响。
  • 几个号同时用。 在选了账号的卡上「打开终端」，这个终端固定用卡片上的号，之后切换卡片不影响它。
  • 套餐和额度。 卡片副标题显示账号和套餐（如 Max 20x），登录需要更新时带一个提示；卡片底部默认显示这个号的剩余额度，授权中心里每个号也有一样的额度列。不在用的号令牌过期时，CC Switch 在它的目录里运行一次 Claude Code 自带的 claude -p /usage（每个号最多 10 分钟一次），由 Claude Code 自己刷新登录、查询额度，CC Switch 只读结果。/usage 不调用模型，不消耗对话额度。没选账号的官方卡也会显示当前登录的套餐。

几点注意：

  • 切号后正在用的 Remote Control 会断开；换号后第一条消息要重建提示缓存，会慢一点、多用一点额度。
  • 在设置里覆盖了 Claude 配置目录（包括指向 WSL）时，切卡不会切换登录，界面会提示改用「打开终端」。

Codex 的模型列表可以拖动排序

Codex 供应商表单里的模型列表，每行开头有拖动手柄，可以用鼠标或键盘调整顺序（#8870）。这个顺序就是 Codex 里 /model 菜单的顺序。第一行代表默认模型时，拖到第一行的模型成为新的默认模型。以前要删掉再加回来才能挪位置。

改进

「打开终端」挪到卡片上。 以前在 ⋯ 菜单里，现在是「编辑」左边的一个图标按钮。悬停提示是「以此供应商打开终端」，终端固定用这张卡时（填了自己的地址和 Key 的卡、选了 Claude 账号的官方卡）会写「（环境隔离）」。

授权中心每组的「添加账号」按钮固定在标题右边。 以前没有账号时按钮会挪到空白提示里，几组卡片的按钮高低不一。ChatGPT 的登录方式改成按钮旁边的一个小下拉。标题里的「删除全部账号」菜单去掉了，账号在各自那一行的菜单里删除。各个账号的额度条现在对齐，长度一致。

轻量模式重启后保持。 在轻量模式下退出 CC Switch，下次启动仍是轻量模式，不再弹出主界面。从 ccswitch:// 链接冷启动时照常打开主界面，好显示导入对话框。

Hermes 卡片显示「默认」，「设为默认」移到 ⋯ 菜单里。 Hermes 把添加的供应商都写进 config.yaml，当前那家只是默认值，和 OpenClaw、OpenCode、Kimi Code 一样。以前的电源图标「启用」按钮看起来像开关。

卡片变成「使用中」时，用量一栏不再左右挪动。

MCP 选了 SSE 时提示哪些应用不支持（#8882）。Codex 只支持 stdio 和 HTTP，会把 SSE 地址当作 HTTP 连接，服务端只支持 SSE 时连不上；Pi 和 Antigravity 保存时会报错。现在「写入到」下面会提前提示。

Claude Desktop 的上游地址填 githubcopilot.com 时，上游格式显示为「由路由按模型自动选择」（#8838），和选 GitHub Copilot 供应商时一样。

OpenCode 的 OMO、OMO Slim 卡片不再显示「检测连通」（#8842）。它们只是代理和模型的对应关系，没有地址可测，以前点了会报错。

土耳其语翻译补全（#8836）。

预设： DeepSeek 一律改用 Flash 模型；Kimi 官方预设加入 K3.1 Pro 和 K3.1 Pro HighSpeed，可以选 low / high / max 思考程度（#8867）；OpenClaw 的 MiniMax M3.1 Flash 可以选 max；新增 ClinePass（#8885）、TeamoRouter Claude（#8266）；Claude Desktop 的 OpenCode Go 改用官方文档的地址，额度也能查了（#8720、#8779）。

修复

  • 托管 ChatGPT 账号重启后显示「登录已过期」、不能切换，过一会儿又自己好了（#8901）。以前刷新登录时遇到任何 HTTP 403 都当成登录失效，网络错误也显示成过期。现在和 Codex CLI 一样，只有 401 或明确的令牌失效错误才算过期；一时的网络问题照常重试，额度保留上一次的结果。托盘切换后，主界面里查询失败的额度也会重新查询。
  • Codex 经本地路由时，请求发到了别的地址（#8849、#8848）。config.toml 里有多个供应商表、或上面有一行注释掉的旧地址时，以前取的是文本里第一个 base_url，会带着选中供应商的 Key 发到别处。现在按 model_provider 选中的那张表取地址。
  • Codex 远程压缩的用量有时漏记（#8879）。
  • Codex 的 MCP 配置里多出 timeout、autoApprove，Codex 桌面版报未知设置，--strict-config 拒绝加载（#8843）。这两个字段是别的客户端的，以前原样写进了 config.toml，手动删掉下次同步又会写回来。现在写给 Codex 时去掉，其他应用不受影响。
  • Codex 经中转连到多个 Azure 资源时，会话中途报 400「created under a different Azure OpenAI resource」，无法继续（#8877）。现在收到这个错误后重试一次，去掉历史记录的条目 ID。正常的请求不受影响。
  • ClinePass 这类把 Chat Completions 结果包在 data 里返回的上游，非流式请求报 422「No choices in response」（#5658）。
  • npm 12 下，「应用」页装的 OpenCode 在 Windows 上报「与你运行的 Windows 版本不兼容」，Grok Build 升级后仍在跑旧版本（#8863）。npm 12 默认不运行安装脚本，这两个包要靠它放置真正的程序。现在安装和升级时允许它们的安装脚本。
  • Windows 上 Hermes 在「应用」页显示成两处安装（#8839）。同一目录里遗留的 hermes.cmd 不再算一处安装。
  • Kimi Code 的用量，一整轮对话的请求时间都一样，按小时统计时挤在最后一小时（#8899）。现在按每次请求完成的时间记。已导入的记录在会话文件下次变化时会更正。
  • Oh My Pi 的 models.yml 里有 name: Search & Fetch 这类含 &、*、! 的值时，保存供应商时报「此 YAML 使用了 CC Switch 无法安全编辑的格式或语法」（#8897）。
  • ~/.gemini/.env 里写成 export GEMINI_API_KEY=... 时，导入后没有 Key，切回默认供应商还会把它从文件里删掉（#8862、#8861）。
  • 从启用了提示词的项目切到没有提示词的项目，原来的提示词没有停用。
  • 用量统计页展开或收起侧栏时，趋势图右边缘跳动（#8548）。

致谢

感谢 @L4XB（Codex 路由地址、Gemini export 写法）、@syxscott（Codex 远程压缩用量）、@jlxzl（Claude Desktop 的 OpenCode Go 地址）、@Railgun-wiki（Claude Desktop 的 Copilot 地址识别）、@VipCrazyMaster（OMO 卡片的连通检测）、@allenxu09（用量趋势图）、@alkank（土耳其语翻译）、@2867297362mjb-sketch（TeamoRouter 预设）、@zayokami（路由端到端测试）、@Ming-Sir-69（Windows 编译修复）、@SaladDay（issue 与 PR 模板、CI）的贡献。

感谢 @chunliang7258、@Lightly20110815、@Catcaca11j、@xovow、@Haoxuan1016、@karenepitaya、@lwdreamroom、@QingYunA、@bamboodew、@Little-TBS、@sojo-negai、@ppdragon2026、@smart-wcm、@oscarachiang 报告问题、提供复现信息和修复思路。

特别感谢 @octo-patch：你的 PR 发现并修复了真实的问题，只是同样的问题后来在主线上另行修好，PR 因此关闭。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。

cline/cline ↗ 4 releases · 4 stable
v4.1.26 Stable2026-10-26T09:06:50Z

Added

  • Cloud tasks: Settings > API Configuration has a Local | Cloud toggle when cloud sessions are on. Cloud picks the Cline model new cloud tasks run on, saved separately from your local models. Clicking the model label in a cloud session's composer opens it there.
  • Starting or resuming a cloud sandbox shows a progress card with the repo, branch and steps (start sandbox, clone repository, start agent). It collapses once the agent starts.

Changed

  • Cloud sessions hide the Plan/Act toggle and the auto-approve bar instead of showing them disabled.
  • With no model chosen, the Cline provider uses Claude Sonnet 5.5 again, which is back at the top of Cline's recommended list.
  • Model requests retry at most 3 times instead of 5.
  • Kimi K3 now gets max reasoning for Extra High and Max, instead of an xhigh setting it rejects.
  • Refreshed the model catalog. Adds the CommandCode, Reflection, Liquid Inference and Munito AI providers and drops CrofAI, iFlow and Llama. The Cline free list adds Reflection Beam, Nemotron 3.5 Lightning and Laguna S 2.1 and drops Mimo V2.6 Flash and Muse Spark 1.3 Contributor. ClinePass now defaults to Kimi K3.1 Pro and adds MiniMax M3.1 Flash and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017. Default models change for most other providers, mostly to Kimi K3.1 Pro, MiniMax M3.1 Flash or DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017. If you use a provider without pinning a model, expect a different default.

Fixed

  • GPT-6 models (such as gpt-6-sol and gpt-6.1-sol) work on OpenAI and Azure OpenAI-compatible endpoints, and SAP AI Core no longer rejects requests with "Unsupported parameter: 'max_tokens'". Both now get max_completion_tokens.
  • Gemini API keys (the gemini provider) no longer send native Google Search. Free-tier keys failed every turn with a 429 quota error on Gemini 3+, and on Gemini 2.x every Cline tool was dropped.
  • Toggling Plan/Act while a task is starting no longer drops your first prompt, and the session no longer keeps running with the other mode's tools.
  • Disabled rules stay disabled.
  • Starting a command no longer moves keyboard focus into the terminal, so text you're typing in the chat no longer lands in the shell.
  • Cancelling a task or opening another one while Cline is asking a question no longer lets your next message get taken as the answer to the old question.
  • A turn whose connection drops after the model has streamed only reasoning is now retried instead of ending the task.
  • Auto-compaction no longer runs for models with an unknown context limit, and it no longer runs again every few tool calls after the first compaction.
  • ask_question: the model keeps going after you pick an option, and empty answers are rejected.
  • run_commands with an empty command list now returns an error instead of a silent success. On PowerShell, the tool tells the model how quote escaping works and to confirm recursive deletes first.
  • Task header badges wrap instead of hiding the close button, the GitHub connection check no longer flashes a loading indicator, and the Local/Cloud toggle's padding is fixed.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/cline/cline/compare/v4.1.25...v4.1.26

View release →

desktop-v0.0.49Desktop v0.0.49 Stable2026-10-26T04:49:34Z
  • A new Changes button in the session header shows the session's line counts and opens a pane listing the files changed in the last turn or across the whole session, with a diff of each. When a turn finishes, a summary under it lists the files it changed; click it to open the pane on that turn
  • A project files panel (folder button in the session header) shows a file tree beside the conversation, with a read-only tabbed viewer, git status letters, and a marker on files the session changed. Files the agent edited get a File/Diff toggle. Works on local and SSH environments
  • An integrated terminal panel opens at the bottom of the window (toggle with Ctrl+backtick)
  • Pull request status moved from the bar above the composer into a header button. Its menu shows the PR title, merge status and checks (failing checks first), and lets you open the PR on GitHub, create a PR, copy the branch name, or refresh
  • A new Local Models settings page manages Ollama and LM Studio models: setup guidance, model details and storage, and a composer chip showing how much memory the loaded model uses
  • Keybindings are configurable in Settings (also reachable from the app and Help menus). Quit can require holding the shortcut, 1 second by default on macOS, so it's harder to quit by accident
  • Slash commands and @file mentions show as chips in the composer and in sent messages
  • If the Hub fails to start, the app now shows a startup screen with Retry and Export diagnostics, instead of failing every action with "desktop backend endpoint not ready"
  • General settings shows the Hub's status and has a Restart Hub button
  • Opening a task that's already running in another Cline app now attaches to that run instead of starting a second one that couldn't be stopped. Requests that were waiting on an app that closed mid-task now fail with a clear message instead of hanging
  • Sessions with a very large transcript (over about 8 MB) can be resumed again. Before, the transcript was sent to the Hub twice, which went over its message size limit
  • Long lines in the file and diff viewers wrap, and rows always span the full width when scrolled
  • New Cloud chats start on your local Cline or ClinePass model. If Cloud doesn't offer that model, ClinePass subscribers get a ClinePass model and everyone else gets Claude Sonnet 5.5
  • A pasted Cline API key now replaces an old browser sign-in instead of being ignored. Provider settings fields stack properly in the narrow side panel
  • The Sessions/Projects sort choice in the sidebar is remembered across restarts
  • On Linux desktops without a tray (stock GNOME on Fedora or Debian), closing the window quits the app instead of leaving it running with no way to quit
  • Internal messages meant only for the model no longer show in the transcript, and live session titles no longer show the raw user_input wrapper
  • Schedule status stays current and failed runs show the full error
  • Checking for changes no longer spawns thousands of git processes in workspaces with many untracked files
  • SSH environments behind a ProxyJump alias now connect
  • The update toast's Restart button sits under the message text
  • A turn whose connection drops after the model has streamed only reasoning is now retried instead of ending the run
  • Model requests retry at most 3 times instead of 5
  • Kimi K3 now gets max reasoning for Extra High and Max, instead of an xhigh setting it rejects
  • Gemini API keys (the gemini provider) no longer send native Google Search. Free-tier keys failed every turn with a 429 quota error on Gemini 3+, and on Gemini 2.x every Cline tool was dropped
  • Auto-compaction no longer runs for models with an unknown context limit, and it no longer runs again every few tool calls after the first compaction
  • ask_question: the model keeps going after you pick an option, empty answers are rejected, and an answer with only an attachment counts
  • run_commands with an empty command list now returns an error instead of a silent success. On PowerShell, the tool tells the model how quote escaping works and to confirm recursive deletes first
  • Refreshed the model catalog. Adds the Liquid Inference and Munito AI providers and drops CrofAI, iFlow and Llama. The Cline free list adds Reflection Beam, Nemotron 3.5 Lightning and Laguna S 2.1 and drops Mimo V2.6 Flash and Muse Spark 1.3 Contributor. ClinePass now defaults to Kimi K3.1 Pro and adds MiniMax M3.1 Flash and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017. Default models change for 56 other providers, mostly to Kimi K3.1 Pro, MiniMax M3.1 Flash or DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017

Full Changelog: https://github.com/cline/cline/compare/desktop-v0.0.48...desktop-v0.0.49

cli-v3.0.74CLI v3.0.74 Stable2026-10-26T04:03:28Z
  • Opening a task that's already running in another Cline app now attaches to that run instead of starting a second one that couldn't be stopped. If an app closes mid-task, requests waiting on it now fail with a clear message instead of hanging
  • cline hub status and cline doctor no longer hang on a Hub that has frozen. Its health check now times out after 3s and the Hub is reported as not running
  • A turn whose connection drops after the model has streamed only reasoning (for example, "The socket connection was closed unexpectedly") is now retried instead of ending the run
  • Model requests retry at most 3 times instead of 5
  • Kimi K3 now gets max reasoning for Extra High and Max, instead of an xhigh setting it rejects
  • Gemini API keys (the gemini provider) no longer send native Google Search. Free-tier keys failed every turn with a 429 quota error on Gemini 3+, and on Gemini 2.x every Cline tool was dropped
  • Auto-compaction no longer runs for models with an unknown context limit, and it no longer runs again every few tool calls after the first compaction
  • ask_question: the model keeps going after you pick an option, empty answers are rejected, and an answer with only an attachment counts
  • run_commands with an empty command list now returns an error instead of a silent success. On PowerShell, the tool tells the model how quote escaping works and to confirm recursive deletes first
  • Plugin slash commands run in the active session
  • Compiled CLI binaries load plugins and serve the dashboard from resources embedded in the binary
  • ACP: the provider and account options now appear under the right config categories
  • The skills tool call label no longer shows null arguments
  • Refreshed the model catalog. Adds the Liquid Inference and Munito AI providers and drops CrofAI, iFlow and Llama. The Cline free list adds Reflection Beam, Nemotron 3.5 Lightning and Laguna S 2.1 and drops Mimo V2.6 Flash and Muse Spark 1.3 Contributor. ClinePass now defaults to Kimi K3.1 Pro and adds MiniMax M3.1 Flash and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017. Default models change for 56 other providers, mostly to Kimi K3.1 Pro, MiniMax M3.1 Flash or DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017

sdk/sdk/v0.2.0SDK v0.2.0 Stable2026-10-26T03:34:08Z
  • Starting a session that already has a run in progress now reattaches to the live session instead of replacing its runtime. Before, the original run kept going but couldn't be reached, and its transcript writes landed on a stale snapshot. A start that includes a prompt while a run is in progress is rejected
  • The hub records every client that registered a capability for a session, including clients that join a task that's already running, and sends each request to the first one still connected. If none is left, the request fails quickly with a message the user can act on, instead of waiting forever
  • A turn whose connection drops after the model has streamed only reasoning is now retried, and the partial reasoning is discarded. Turns with text, media or tool calls are still never retried
  • Model requests retry at most 3 times after the first attempt (down from 5). Failed attempts are recorded in Langfuse traces, and cancelled requests are no longer reported as Langfuse errors
  • AI SDK updated to ai 7.0.136 / @ai-sdk/provider-utils 5.0.58. max reasoning now reaches the provider: the gateway no longer turns it into xhigh, and Kimi K3 gets max for Extra High and Max. Empty Anthropic turns are still retried despite the new anthropic.* custom stream parts
  • The gemini provider no longer sends native Google Search. On Gemini 3+, free-tier keys failed every turn with a 429 quota error, and on Gemini 2.x it dropped every Cline tool. Vertex keeps native search
  • Auto-compaction no longer runs when the model's input limit is unknown. Manual and overflow-recovery compaction still work
  • After the first compaction, the size estimate now uses the messages as they are actually sent, with tool results truncated. Before, it overestimated about 7x and compaction ran again every few tool calls
  • Checkpoint diffs read file contents with a single git cat-file --batch process instead of up to two git show processes per file. Workspaces with many untracked files used to spawn thousands of git processes
  • SSH remotes work behind a ProxyJump alias. The connect budget goes from 10s to 30s, the per-command limit to 60s, and tunnel readiness to 40s. When ssh exits mid-upload, its real error is reported instead of EPIPE, and a LocalForward on the alias no longer collides with the tunnel
  • ask_question answers are framed so the model keeps going after a bare choice. Empty answers are rejected, and an answer with only an attachment counts as answered
  • run_commands rejects an empty commands list with a tool error, and its schema now advertises minItems: 1
  • On PowerShell, the run_commands description explains backtick escaping, single-quoting literal paths, preferring cmdlets over cmd /c, and confirming recursive deletes first
  • Plugin commands run in the active session runtime and the hub. Plugin watches start working again when discovery directories appear
  • Compiled binaries load embedded plugin and dashboard resources straight from the binary, and every declared SDK runtime export is packaged
  • A configured CLINE_PLUGIN_RUNTIME_DIR is resolved to an absolute path
  • When OpenRouter has no match for a Cline recommended model, its capabilities are filled in from the Vercel catalog
  • Desktop cloud session orchestration moved into the hub. session.list reports activeCloudCommands, and the hub isn't replaced while cloud commands are still running
  • Core adds conversation summaries and cloud continuation support
  • Run stall telemetry: agent.run-finished and agent.run-failed carry stall signals from a new RunHealthObserver
  • @cline/ui now ships with the SDK packages on the shared version. It adds a Kbd component and token chips for slash commands and file mentions in AgentComposer
  • Refreshed the model catalog (7,041 → 6,457 models). Adds the Liquid Inference, Munito AI and Munito AI (EU) providers and drops CrofAI, iFlow and Llama. The Cline free list adds Reflection Beam, Nemotron 3.5 Lightning and Laguna S 2.1 and drops Mimo V2.6 Flash and Muse Spark 1.3 Contributor. ClinePass adds Kimi K3.1 Pro (now its default), MiniMax M3.1 Flash and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017, and drops Mimo V2.5 and V2.5 Pro. Default models change for 56 other providers, mostly to Kimi K3.1 Pro, MiniMax M3.1 Flash or DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017

Full Changelog: https://github.com/cline/cline/compare/sdk/sdk/v0.0.95...sdk/sdk/v0.2.0

cua-spaces-v0.16.0Cua Spaces v0.16.0 Stable2026-10-25T21:36:59Z

Cua Spaces 0.16.0

Summary

This release adds 3 features and includes 14 fixes.

Features

  • link System Status from Help, Settings About and the troubleshoot guide (CUA-2228). (#5902)
  • new app icon, the terracotta koala keycap. (#5914)
  • onboarding v2 PostHog funnel events (CUA-2292). (#5951)

Fixes

  • submit again when notarytool crashes before it returns a submission id. (#5874)
  • the notch counts only running or starting Spaces and collapses at zero. (#5872)
  • uninstall-local.sh also removes the Local preferences, caches and web view data (CUA-2207). (#5880)
  • Cua Spaces Local uses the bundle id com.trycua.spaces.macos.local. (#5884)
  • "Skip for now" sticks across launches, and Local's agent setup names are guarded (CUA-2221). (#5889)
  • Cua Spaces Local's hints and errors name cua-local, not cua. (#5892)
  • uninstall-local.sh takes this Mac off the relay and Local's entries out of your coding agents (CUA-2212, CUA-2222). (#5882)
  • dogfooding polish (welcome copy first). (#5893)
  • app icon gets its own charcoal background, so macOS 26 stops adding a grey plate (CUA-2244). (#5924)
  • the permissions guide closes itself once access is granted (CUA-2274). (#5927)
  • the web page's usage signals reach the SwiftUI app before Settings is opened (CUA-2291). (#5953)
  • sign-in that never comes back offers a code (CUA-2291). (#5950)
  • signing in as another account recovers the daemon session, host registration and mesh key (CUA-2295). (#5961)
  • never show an empty Spaces page while computers are still answering. (#5905)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

longbridge/gpui-kit ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.8.1 Stable2026-10-26T08:27:19Z

GPUI Kit v0.8.1

GPUI Kit 0.8.1 improves text selection, Markdown rendering, editor layout, scrolling, focus and accessibility. It also adds compatible customization APIs for text rendering, editor scrollbars and Diff parsing and highlighting.

To preserve Kit-owned API compatibility with v0.8.0, this patch defers the MotionTokens field additions from #3616 and the Textarea associated-type change from #2842. Behavior fixes remain included. The default backend is pinned to gpui-pre 0.3.10, and the optional GPUI Fast backend uses 0.1.12. These upstream snapshots change several GPUI APIs re-exported by Kit; see the migration notes below.

Editor and text input

  • Tab stops: code-editor tabs align to the configured tab stops during measurement, wrapping and painting. Horizontal scroll extents follow laid-out text widths. (#3622, #3604)
  • Custom scrollbars: set_scrollbar_renderer lets an application supply its editor scrollbar presentation using EditorScrollbarContext. (#3644)
  • Focused inputs ask surrounding scroll containers to reveal them, taps request the software keyboard, and accessibility semantics follow the actual editor focus. (#3625, #3623, #3585)

Markdown and text rendering

  • Selection: publish selection snapshots once per frame to avoid invalidating unrelated retained views. Double- and triple-click drags and subsequent Shift-clicks extend selections by words, lines or paragraphs, including paragraphs with hard breaks. Drags starting inside a table cell stay in that cell. (#3686, #3681, #3646)
  • Text styles: inline_code_style, list_style and blockquote_style refine the corresponding text presentation while inheriting unspecified properties from the resolved style. (#3663, #3687)
  • Image sources: Component TextView exposes image_source to resolve document images consistently for measurement and rendering. (#3694)
  • Preserve sparse Markdown table rows and release shaped text retained by dropped TextViews on the next frame. (#3673, #3628)

Diff

  • Truncated patches: DiffFile::parse_with and DiffParseOptions::truncated_hunks accept incomplete hunks without relaxing the default parser. DiffFile::is_truncated identifies affected files; unified file boundaries and empty-hunk ordering remain validated. (#3688)
  • Syntax highlighting: with_syntax_highlighter, set_syntax_highlighter and clear_syntax_highlighter let an application provide its own highlighting without resetting selection or scrolling. (#3618)

Scrolling and layout

  • Scrollable regions can scroll content beyond their maximum size. Bounce handling avoids rounding-induced overscroll and false overscroll at large offsets, and release inertia applies only to stretched edges. (#3613, #3632, #3661, #3631)
  • last_horizontal_scroll_claim exposes the last horizontal scroll step consumed by a mask. Resizable dividers read the active drag state when events arrive. (#3621, #3629)
  • on_prepaint reports bounds from the element's corner, aligning geometry callbacks with their intended coordinate origin. (#3689)

Components

  • Dialog: Dialog::centered and AlertDialog::centered center the surface vertically; exit animation matches entrance animation. Sheets and notifications avoid system bars and the software keyboard. (#3635, #3603, #3624)
  • Button: track_focus accepts an application-owned handle. Disabling a focused button preserves the focus binding so Tab can still leave it in either direction. (#3684, #3606)
  • DataTable: honor centered and right-aligned columns; expose table structure and cell labels to assistive technology through TableDelegate::cell_label. (#3617, #3667)
  • Toolbar: grow with taller content and preserve focus when blank toolbar space is clicked. (#3651, #3652)
  • HoverCard: trigger_style applies layout styling to the trigger container. (#3653)
  • ColorPicker: preserve the first hex edit after a programmatic value update and round color channels instead of truncating them. (#3641, #3640)
  • TimeField: pressing outside a segment focuses the first segment. Command applies empty-list padding only while its empty state is visible. (#3655, #3654)
  • Focus trap: stop Tab traversal at the first repeated stop. Dock restores legacy empty StackPanel layouts as empty splits. (#3642, #3610)

Shell and localization

The release also prepares gpui-shell for crates.io publication with matching quickjs-jit, quickjs-jit-runtime and quickjs-jit-stdlib 0.14.1 registry dependencies.

  • Shell Button: accessibility_label supplies an accessible name independently of the visible label. (#3665)
  • Add Brazilian Portuguese translations and preserve valid Portuguese words in spell checking. (#3638, #3664)

Upstream GPUI compatibility

The updated GPUI snapshots change public APIs re-exported at the Kit root. Internal compilation checks and Kit API checks do not preserve these upstream signatures:

  • Modifiers::secondary() and Modifiers::secondary_key() now require &App.
  • Keystroke::parse and KeyBinding::new require static strings. Dynamic strings and secondary modifiers use OS-aware parsing/loading.
  • Keystroke::unparse and KeybindingKeystroke::unparse require an OperatingSystem argument.
  • PlatformInput::ScrollWheel becomes PlatformInput::Scroll; scroll event literals gain a source field.

Use cx.platform_info().os for OS-aware parsing/formatting and pass cx to modifier helpers. Use KeyBinding::load(..., cx) for dynamic keybindings or choose explicit cmd/ctrl literals with KeyBinding::new.

Thank you to everyone who contributed code, documentation, testing, and feedback!

What's Changed

  • dock: Read the legacy empty StackPanel as an empty split by @obito-t in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3610

vercel-labs/agent-browser ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.39.4 Stable2026-10-26T05:50:52Z

Bug Fixes

  • Fixed wait --download timing out when the download finished before the wait started, and moving the file to the requested path (#2027)
  • Fixed --allowed-domains launches failing with "Cannot find default execution context" on pages that embed cross-origin iframes (#2116)
  • Fixed full-page screenshots on high-DPI displays being twice the page size with the page in the top-left quarter (#2125)
  • Fixed selector screenshots being blank for elements outside the first viewport, after scrolling, or inside iframes (#2126)
  • Fixed unresponsive tabs permanently breaking a session; a wedged tab now fails with page_wedged and, in a launched browser, is replaced after a second timeout (#2100)
  • Fixed --pin-tab so another session cannot take a pinned tab with tab <targetId> (#2135)

v0.39.3 Stable2026-10-26T01:37:06Z

Bug Fixes

  • Fixed auth encryption keys being overwritten when the configured key or existing key file is invalid, preserving access to previously saved credentials (#2178)
  • Fixed project config trust so auto-discovered files cannot set executable paths, browser arguments, or plugins; plugin add now writes the user config by default (#2180)
  • Fixed MCP access limits so tool arguments cannot override the server's network, file, policy, or host access settings (#2181)
  • Fixed inspect WebSocket origins so browser connections must come from the local Chrome DevTools origin (#2182)
  • Fixed stale process cleanup so daemon restarts, close --all, and dashboard shutdown check process identity before sending signals (#2183)
  • Fixed copied Chrome profiles so their saved preferences mark the copy as cleanly closed, avoiding repeated crash-restore prompts while retaining saved tabs (#2189)
  • Fixed dashboard chat sessions so commands stay in their thread's session, background turns remain attached to that session, and closed sessions stop their chat (#2190)
  • Fixed Windows output capture so detached daemons and dashboards do not hold the CLI's output pipes open, and MCP output draining is bounded after child exit (#2160)

Improvements

  • Improved CDP startup attachment so connect and --cdp wait up to two seconds for a debugging port that is still refusing connections (#2159)

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-10-26T05:27:19Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-1952+g187d13aa60
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1792527110

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

stablyai/orca ↗ 2 releases · 1 stable · 1 prerelease
mobile-android-v0.0.59Orca Mobile Android mobile-android-v0.0.59 Prerelease2026-10-26T04:21:00Z

Thank you for using Orca mobile! ❤️

What's new

  • Opening Orca after your computer updates is about twice as fast. Downloads are decoded faster, and only the files that changed are fetched instead of the whole app.
  • The download progress bar now fills smoothly instead of jumping to the end.
  • Going back to a screen you already have open takes you there instead of opening another copy.
  • Terminal output that the phone sometimes dropped, such as the screen reset after a full-screen program exits, now shows up.

Most phone features come from your desktop, so update Orca on your computer to get everything. See the desktop release notes for the full list.

Verify your download

  • Size: 136281689 bytes
  • SHA-256: f8a18086b04dc00966431edc4d484132d6fc8dc08a5d88009a3246317a12f343

A "no certificate" or "problem parsing the package" install failure is usually a truncated download — check the size first, then sha256sum -c app-release.apk.sha256 (shasum -a 256 -c on macOS).

v1.4.241 Stable2026-10-26T03:48:28Z

Chats and a browser that behave the way you expect 🧭

This release makes chats follow your agent settings, brings Chrome-style tools to the built-in browser, and helps SSH connections and managed servers recover. You’ll also find a chat font setting, live agent browsing, and steadier Hermes, Kimi, Kiro and Windows terminals.

The short version

  • Chats that follow your settings: New chats use each agent’s permission from Settings → Agents, renamed chats keep their names in Session History, and a chat won’t write a conversation a terminal is still running.
  • A browser closer to Chrome: Dock DevTools inside the pane, mute tabs playing sound, see a link’s URL on hover, and use Orca shortcuts and mouse back/forward inside pages.
  • Watch agents browse: A cursor and badge show where an agent is clicking or typing, chat jumps you to the page, and links from terminals and chat open beside their source.
  • SSH with fewer dead ends: Saved port forwards return after a restart and explain failures, certificate-only hosts connect through your system ssh, and managed servers update without closing terminals.
  • Agents that start and resume reliably: Hermes and Kimi panes resume after a restart, Kiro reports status on its V3 engine, and keys typed while an agent starts are kept as a draft.
  • Polish where it counts: Pick a font for chat, zoom with Cmd/Ctrl + + on non-US keyboards, open cloud-only files from macOS terminals, and get steadier Windows terminals and pasting.

Phone & web updates: Phones no longer drop pieces of terminal output, and chat renames and each chat’s permission mode now show there. Session tabs also keep Working and Needs You for Windows agents that never set a title.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • Fresh terminal host: New terminals start on a fresh terminal host so this release’s terminal fixes apply. Terminals already open before you update keep running, and stay attached, on the previous host until you close them.
  • Chat permissions: Settings → Chat no longer has its own permission dropdown; set it per agent in Settings → Agents. Existing chats keep their saved permission, and a chat’s own permission menu changes only that chat.
  • Conversations open in a terminal: If a terminal might still be writing a conversation, its chat won’t send and asks you to quit the agent there or close that terminal. Closing that terminal lets the next send go through.
  • Managed SSH servers: Update anyway keeps running terminals going through the update. The offer to resume chats after a restart needs a server already running this release.
  • OpenCode model picks: OpenCode 2.0.14, 2.0.15, 2.0.17 and 2.0.18 leave the first message unsent when a model is picked, so Orca refuses model picks on those versions.

Product experience

FlashML-org/FreeToken ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.2.1 Stable2026-10-26T03:24:21Z

Highlights

  • [Feature] Image input for GGUF models. A llama.cpp mmproj file next to the model, or one passed with --mmproj, adds image input to Qwen3.5 / Qwen3.6 / Qwen3.8, Gemma-4, GLM-5.3-Flash and DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision-Exp GGUF files (#803, #810, #816).
  • [Feature] More GGUF archs. deepseek4 (DeepSeek-V4 Flash, Flash-0731, Pro, Pro-0813) (#774), qwen3vl and qwen3vlmoe (text only) (#807).
  • [Feature] Support Flint, from BF16 safetensors, the ModelOpt NVFP4 export or the official NVFP4 GGUF (#797).
  • [Feature] Embedding table in host RAM. --embed-weights host keeps the input token embedding table in pinned host RAM and gives its VRAM to the expert cache and KV: decode is 8.7% faster on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B with 24 GiB of free VRAM (#806).

GGUF Support

  • [Feature] Image input from an mmproj file (#803). llama.cpp keeps the vision tower in a separate mmproj-*.gguf. Without --mmproj, FreeToken takes a *mmproj*.gguf that fits the model (output width, tower type, image tokens) from the model file's own folder, BF16 before F32, F16 and quantized files; the log names the file it loads. With none, or with files of several models it cannot tell apart, the model serves text only; a repo that keeps each quant in a subfolder and the mmproj at the top needs --mmproj. An mmproj in the model folder turns image input on; --text-model-only (or --mm-disable vision) serves text only as before. The tower always runs in bf16, so a quantized mmproj is dequantized at load. See Image input.

    Family GGUF arch mmproj projector PR
    Qwen3.5, Qwen3.6, Qwen3.8-27B qwen35, qwen35moe qwen3vl_merger #803
    Qwen3.8-Flash-Next qwen4exp qwen3vl_merger #803
    Gemma-4 26B-A4B, 31B gemma4 gemma4v #803
    Gemma-4 12B (audio tensors are skipped) gemma4 gemma4uv #803
    GLM-5.3-Flash glm5-next glm5v #810
    DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision-Exp deepseek4 deepseek4v #816

  • [Feature] deepseek4 arch: DeepSeek-V4 Flash, Flash-0731, Pro, Pro-0813, and the Vision-Exp text file. Routed experts keep the ggml types they ship with and run the clamped SwiGLU. A file without a rope.scaling block gets the family's YaRN values with a warning; llama.cpp serves such files unscaled (#774)

  • [Feature] qwen3vl, qwen3vlmoe archs: Qwen3-VL dense and MoE, text only (#807)

  • [Feature] flint arch: Flint (#797)

  • [Feature] NVFP4 token_embd tables load (#801). A table with a .scale sidecar is still refused, as llama.cpp reads no scale for it.

  • [Fix] Qwen GGUF files (llama.cpp pre-tokenizer qwen2 or qwen35) apply the NFC normalization of Qwen's tokenizer.json, so text with combining accents tokenizes as with the HF tokenizer (#807)

  • Not supported yet: image input on qwen3vl / qwen3vlmoe files; GGUF files that carry the vision tensors themselves (the Ollama layout), which serve text only; image input on DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision-Exp text GGUFs converted before llama.cpp #28154 (no exp_probs_b_vl; reconvert); image requests on ggml-org's DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision-Exp text GGUF and its re-uploads, whose chat template renders no image placeholder (serve unsloth's release for images); FTWs converted from a GGUF, which stay text-only.

Model Support

  • [Feature] Support Flint (FlintForCausalLM) from BF16 safetensors, the ModelOpt NVFP4 export and the official NVFP4 GGUF; the glm47 tool parser and the flint reasoning parser are picked automatically, and ModelOpt NVFP4 experts run on offload, cpu or hybrid (#797)
  • [Fix] Take the weight files from model.safetensors.index.json when the checkpoint has one, so shards in a subdirectory (nvidia's *-FP4-Local releases) are found; such checkpoints found no weight files and failed later with an unrelated error (#819)

Engine

  • [Feature] --embed-weights gpu|host (default gpu): host keeps the input token embedding table in pinned host RAM, and each step reads its rows over PCIe. It is refused when the table is tied to the LM head or is not a plain table (DF11 GLM-4.x) (#806)
  • [Perf] hybrid: the expert-fetch kernel picks cache victims in 8192-slot tiles, so one fetch on a 23,197-slot pool spends 7.7 µs in it instead of 76.9 µs (#778)
  • [Fix] hybrid: fix a race in the route rewrite of the expert-fetch kernel (#778)
  • [Feature] FREETOKEN_HYBRID_FETCH_ROUND chooses how many misses a hybrid decode step fetches: min-wait (default, the previous rule), stochastic or prefer-fetch (#778)
  • [Perf] hybrid with block-FP8 experts: routes that the CPU runs no longer also run on the GPU with weight 0; with 2 of 8 routes on the GPU, the GPU side of a decode MoE layer is about 3x faster (kernel benchmark on an RTX A1000) (#665)
  • [Fix] The --moe-prefill-hit-d2d batch-copy probe waits for its buffer fill, so a busy GPU no longer fails the probe and turns --moe-prefill-hit-d2d off (#771)
  • [Perf] GLM-5.3-Flash DSA prefill drops redundant activation copies: the attention peak of a 12,288-token chunk goes from 4,473 to 1,749 MiB, which removes the allocator warnings reported in #770 (#795)
  • [Fix] DSA sparse attention (GLM-5.2, GLM-5.3-Flash) stops with a message to lower --max-prefill-length when a prefill chunk is past int32 addressing (about 65k tokens), instead of an illegal memory access (#795)
  • [Docs] New guide, performance/prefill.md: find whether prefill with offloaded experts is bound by the PCIe copy or by compute, and what to change (#785)

Kernels

  • [Perf] Route glm4_moe, glm_moe_dsa, glm5_next, minimax_m2 and minimax_m3 (sigmoid scores with a selection-only bias) through one Triton kernel, fused_topk_sigmoid, with the router logits in fp32 as SGLang and vLLM do: decode is 9.1% faster on MiniMax-M2.5-NVFP4 (H100, offload) (#804)
  • [Fix] GLM-5.2 checkpoints and MiniMax-M2 GGUF files no longer round their F32 selection bias to bf16; the fp32 MiniMax-M2 gate takes 93 MiB more GPU memory (#804)
  • [Fix] On sm_89 (RTX 40xx, L4, RTX 6000 Ada), quantize fp32 to e4m3 in one rounding. Triton before 3.9 rounds through fp16 there and moved some block-FP8 and per-tensor FP8 activations one e4m3 step toward zero (#805)
  • [Fix] Keep fp32 inputs intact in the torch mRoPE fallbacks, which rotated the second half from already-rotated values (#641)

Server and CLI

  • [Feature] GET / POST /v1/cache/flush?timeout=<seconds> drops every prefix-cache entry while the server is idle, without reallocating pools or recapturing CUDA graphs, so an eval harness can start each case from an empty cache without a restart. A busy server answers 503; with timeout it waits up to that long for idle and answers 504 if still busy. TP > 1 is not supported yet (#811)
  • [Feature] --mmproj FILE picks the mmproj of a GGUF model (#803)

AMD ROCm (WIP, experimental)

  • [Perf] --moe-prefill-hit-d2d works on ROCm, through hipMemcpyBatchAsync: cached experts are copied on the device instead of over PCIe. Time to first token at 512 prompt tokens drops 34% on the W7900D and 29% on the R9700 (Qwen3.5-35B-A3B-FP8, 40% of experts cached) (#771)
  • [Perf] Disk PLE (Qwen3.8-Flash-Next) and the DeepSeek-V4.1 Engram tables sync with graph replay through HIP stream memory operations (wait-sync) instead of launch gating: decode is 12.7% faster on the R9700 and 0.8% on the W7900D (Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-FP8) (#776)

Breaking Changes & Deprecations

  • GLM-5.2 FTWs converted by older builds load without an error but route with a bf16-rounded selection bias: bf16 keeps only 3 to 8 of nvidia/GLM-5.2-NVFP4's 256 bias values per layer in the layers checked. Restore the fp32 values in place with python scripts/ftw_hotfix.py --ftw <FTW dir> --repo nvidia/GLM-5.2-NVFP4 (or --source <checkpoint dir>); see docs/ftw-hotfix.md (#804).
  • A GGUF model with a fitting *mmproj*.gguf in its folder now builds the vision tower and serves images; --text-model-only restores the text-only footprint (#803).

Contributors

@jason-fxz, @void-mckenzie, @LZ-QWQ, @taking-lying-flat, @iamanishx, @jesusjodar, @ischencheng, @chrisqianz, @zihaomu

First contributions this release:

  • @jesusjodar in #797
  • @chrisqianz in #819

Full changelog: https://github.com/FlashML-org/FreeToken/compare/v0.2.0...v0.2.1

esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v2.52.0Reasonix CLI v2.52.0 Stable2026-10-26T00:35:05Z

CLI archives built from the Studio release studio-v2.52.0.

View release →

studio-v2.52.0Reasonix Studio v2.52.0 Stable2026-10-26T00:30:58Z

本版新增 Wallpaper Engine 场景壁纸、主题包的五个代码配色标记和历史竖带的展开，移除正文拖动把手与顶栏沙盒按钮，并修复压缩卡死、没有文件夹无法开会话等多处问题。

新增

  • 壁纸可以导入 Wallpaper Engine 场景文件夹：提取主图和全部音轨循环播放，支持 DXT1/DXT3/DXT5 压缩主图，提供音量、静音和鼠标视差。关联 #14407 by @EMEEEEMMMM、#14478 by @EMEEEEMMMM。
  • 主题包可以给注释、类型、运算符、标点和变量单独配色（五个可选标记），文件编辑器和对话代码块都生效；不写它们的主题外观不变。关联 #14358 fixed in #14483。
  • 会话折叠设置新增独立的思考开头预览开关：收起时在标题下方单行显示，流式输出时也生效，旧的预览选择自动迁移。关联 #14139。
  • 历史对话竖带里合并成一条的多轮对话，点一下展开成具体每一条再选要跳的那条；历史未全部加载时竖带顶端会提示。关联 #14468 by @imcreazy。

变更

  • 移除正文左右两侧的拖动宽度把手，对话区不再有线条，手机端也不再误触；正文宽度用设置里的行宽预设选择。关联 #14460 fixed in #14463。
  • 顶栏不再常驻「沙盒」按钮，它只是「工具与权限」的快捷入口，该入口已在图标栏和侧栏里，可在导航入口里显示或隐藏。关联 #14439。
  • 把文件拖进会话时，放到会话面板的任意位置都会作为附件或引用加入，不必再精确拖到输入框；侧栏等自带拖放区的位置仍优先。关联 #14443。
  • 文档：用户指南新增「提示词怎么注入」快速说明，区分聊天要求、仓库常驻指令和用户级人设及人设保存后的生效时机。关联 #13512。

修复

  • 没有添加任何文件夹时新建会话不再提示「请先添加一个文件夹」，而是在应用自己的内置工作区里打开。关联 #14363。
  • 大窗口模型下，用户消息很大的长会话不再反复报「折叠后仍不低于压缩阈值」而卡住自动压缩和 /compact。关联 #14434 fixed in #14436。
  • 在 CRLF 文件里按原文编辑时，替换文本里的换行不再以单独的 LF 写入造成混合换行。修复 #14403 fixed in #14404。
  • reasonix serve 先绑定地址再打印启动信息，地址或端口无法使用时只报错，分享链接里的端口是真正在监听的。修复 #14426 fixed in #14429。
  • 放大设置窗口后，内容卡片居中在侧栏右侧的可用区域，左右留白相等。修复 #14418 fixed in #14420。
  • 会话标签里项目文件夹名或主机名很长时，关闭按钮不再被挤到相邻标签上点不到，标题也不再被挤没。关联 #14417 fixed in #14432 by @xxoingr。
  • 模型价格里的币种下拉框与旁边的输入框样式一致，浅色和深色主题都协调。关联 #14380。
  • 设置「工具与权限」里的文件交付、脚本、顾问、多路并行和 MCP 资源读取现在显示中文简介而不是英文原文。关联 #14447。
  • 设置里切换分类会回到页面顶部，搜索结果能落到稍后才加载出来的设置上。关联 #14491 by @SivanCola。
  • 网络设置区分已保存的配置和正在编辑的草稿：没改动时保存按钮不可用，连接测试明确使用已保存的配置。关联 #14490 by @SivanCola。
  • 本机文件备份不再因路由缺失返回 404，导出、预览和应用恢复可用，仍只限本机使用。关联 #14492 by @SivanCola。
  • 取消工具调用后可以退出检查点准入等待；后台任务和子代理舰队的写入登记可取消，收尾与登记绑定，同 ID 的新登记不受旧任务退出影响。关联 #14025。
  • 后台任务即使没被启动也会清理资源，清理出错也可诊断；恢复出来的 panic 包括旧版 nil panic 不再被报告成功。关联 #14025。
  • 工具 hook 可能写文件时先进入检查点写屏障，被拒绝、只读和嵌套的调用也会通知宿主，准入失败则不执行 hook。关联 #14025。
  • 隔离任务的结果由其他会话应用或丢弃后，旧会话不能再靠缓存写回；各操作共享按 ID 的跨进程租约。关联 #14025。
  • 隔离任务首次检查失败或宿主 runner 崩溃时保留文件和原 ID，可排障后重试；整合时按字面文件名和 Git 模式处理，不覆盖你的相邻修改。关联 #14025。
  • 写入事务记录遇到重复字段、大小写别名或未知字段时拒绝新准入并保留原字节，不再被当作普通记录。关联 #14025。
  • 被拒绝的写入会释放临时占用的路径，保留此前写入、并行调用和有效的用户等待。关联 #14025、#14133 by @SivanCola。

升级须知

  • 失败检查记录不按年龄清理；旧版本会保留这些新记录，升级后才能按 ID 重试。关联 #14025。
  • 移除拖动把手后，旧版本记住的拖动宽度不再读取并会在启动时清除；需要时用行宽预设重新选择。关联 #14460 fixed in #14463。

贡献者

感谢本版本的贡献者：@EMEEEEMMMM、@imcreazy、@xxoingr、@SivanCola

<!-- lang:en -->

This release adds Wallpaper Engine scene wallpapers, five code-colour tokens for theme packs and an expandable history rail, removes the reading-column drag handles and the top-bar Sandbox button, and fixes the compaction stall and sessions that would not open without a folder.

New

  • Wallpaper can import a Wallpaper Engine scene folder: its backdrop and every soundtrack loop, DXT1/DXT3/DXT5 backdrops decode, with volume, mute and pointer parallax. Refs #14407 by @EMEEEEMMMM, #14478 by @EMEEEEMMMM.
  • Theme packs can colour comments, types, operators, punctuation and variables with five optional tokens, in the file editor and in code blocks; packs that omit them look the same. Refs #14358 fixed in #14483.
  • An independent collapsed-thinking preview switch shows one line below the header, including while streaming; existing preview choices migrate automatically. Refs #14139.
  • A run of questions folded into one line on the history rail opens on a click so you pick the one you meant, and the rail marks history that is not loaded yet. Refs #14468 by @imcreazy.

Changed

  • The drag handles beside the reading column are removed, so no lines show and a phone cannot hit them; choose the width with the line-width presets in Settings. Refs #14460 fixed in #14463.
  • The top bar no longer has a permanent Sandbox button; Tools and permissions is in the rail and sidebar and can be hidden under Navigation entries. Refs #14439.
  • A file dropped anywhere on the conversation pane is attached or referenced, not only on the composer; drop zones such as the sidebar still win. Refs #14443.
  • Docs: a prompt-customization quick start covers chat, repository rules and user-wide personas, with when a saved persona takes effect. Refs #13512.

Fixed

  • Starting a session with no folder added no longer says to add a folder first; it opens in the app's own workspace. Refs #14363.
  • On large-window models, long sessions with big user messages no longer stall automatic compaction and /compact on "still at or above the threshold". Refs #14434 fixed in #14436.
  • In a CRLF file, a literal edit no longer writes the replacement's line breaks as bare LF and mixes endings. Fixes #14403 fixed in #14404.
  • reasonix serve binds its address before printing anything, so a bad address or port fails with just the error and the share link names a port that is listening. Fixes #14426 fixed in #14429.
  • In the enlarged settings window the content column is centred beside the navigation with equal room on both sides. Fixes #14418 fixed in #14420.
  • A long folder or host name on a session tab no longer pushes the close button onto the next tab or squeezes the title away. Refs #14417 fixed in #14432 by @xxoingr.
  • The currency dropdown in model prices matches the inputs beside it in light and dark themes. Refs #14380.
  • Settings, Tools and permissions now show a blurb in your language for the file-delivery, script, advisor, best-of-N and MCP resource tools. Refs #14447.
  • Switching a settings category returns to the top of the page, and a search result lands on a setting that loads late. Refs #14491 by @SivanCola.
  • Network settings tell the saved configuration from the draft: Save is disabled until something changes, and the connection test uses the saved configuration. Refs #14490 by @SivanCola.
  • Local file backups no longer fail with a 404 for a missing route; export, preview and apply work, still limited to this machine. Refs #14492 by @SivanCola.
  • Cancelling a tool call leaves the checkpoint wait; background writer registrations are cancellable and cleaned up with the job, and a newer one of the same ID survives an old exit. Refs #14025.
  • Background tasks release their resources even when launch is declined, cleanup errors stay visible, and a recovered panic, including legacy nil panics, is never reported as success. Refs #14025.
  • Possible tool-hook writes go through the checkpoint barrier, refused, read-only and nested calls notify the host, and a failed admission does not run the hook. Refs #14025.
  • After another session applies or discards an isolated result, a stale session cannot write it back from a cache; operations share per-ID cross-process leases. Refs #14025.
  • An isolated task keeps its files and ID after a failed first inspection or a runner panic so it can be retried, and integration treats file names literally and keeps your nearby edits. Refs #14025.
  • Mutation records with repeated fields, case aliases or unknown fields are refused for new admission and keep their bytes instead of being treated as ordinary records. Refs #14025.
  • A refused write releases provisional paths while keeping earlier writes, concurrent calls and live user waits. Refs #14025, #14133 by @SivanCola.

Upgrade notes

  • Failed inspection records are not cleaned up by age; older versions keep these new records and need an upgrade to retry them. Refs #14025.
  • With the drag handles gone, a dragged width remembered by an older build is no longer read and is cleared at start; choose a line-width preset if you need to. Refs #14460 fixed in #14463.

Thanks to this release's contributors: @EMEEEEMMMM, @imcreazy, @xxoingr, @SivanCola

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。x86_64 用文件名带 amd64 的包，aarch64（统信 UOS、麒麟等）用带 arm64 的包。也提供 .AppImage（只提供下载，不会自更新；需要 FUSE 2，或用 --appimage-extract-and-run 启动）。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v15.2.1v15.2.1 — npm trusted publishing Stable2026-10-25T19:45:33Z

What changed

  • npm publishing now uses trusted publishing (OIDC). The "Publish to npm" GitHub Actions workflow authenticates to npm with a short-lived OIDC token issued for this repository's workflow instead of the long-lived NPM_TOKEN secret, which had been failing on every tag since v13.26.1. Trusted publishes also carry an npm provenance attestation linking the package to the commit and workflow that built it. (#5063)

No runtime behavior changes in the plugin or worker; this release exists to ship through the new publishing path.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.2.0...v15.2.1

View release →

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v5.1.48 Stable2026-10-25T15:23:25Z

OmO Native stops waiting on a stalled turn-end subscriber after two seconds. Memory recovery remains retryable without treating the cancelled wait as a failed reflection run, and stall diagnostics identify the component and hook instead of only omo.js. Thanks to @floweredao for the report. (#11254, Part of #11249) (#11280)

Warm memory prompts avoid repeated git processes for HEAD and agreements. Committed agreement reads are cached by repository and full revision, and cache misses inspect only the ledger path. Standard file-backed HEADs resolve from stat-validated Git files, including packed refs and linked worktrees, with git fallback for unusual layouts. (#11216, part of #10707) (#11280)

Unchanged memory notices no longer yield a warm prompt to cold recall. Soul and agreement notices read their small atomic watermarks directly and skip lock acquisition only when valid state confirms nothing needs announcing or updating. Missing, malformed, or unreadable state is rechecked under the identity lock. Writers publish through unique, exclusively created temporary files, flush them before rename, and sync the directory where supported. (#11215, part of #10707) (#11280)

Memory notices survive a directory-sync failure after publication. Soul and agreement watermarks still flush before atomic rename and attempt directory sync, but a sync error after publication is logged without discarding that turn's announcement. Failed writes clean up their temporary files, repeated agreement ids use set comparison, and HEAD reads defer to git for worktree configuration. (#11234, part of #10707) (#11280)

Nested in-process tasks retain their original session tree. A child's own tasks now record the tree root and increasing depth, so nested delegation obeys max_depth instead of restarting at depth 1. The root session's own view now also covers its nested in-process descendants: the footer, /tasks, the default task_output list, and task_send and cancel. Process-mode descendants already appeared there. (#11226) (#11280)

Nested team members retain the original session tree. A child session leading a team now gives each member its own session as parent and the original tree root, with depth increasing from the lead. Persisted member ancestry is preserved when rebuilding a respawn. (#11229) (#11280)

The engine moves to senpi 2026.10.25-2: resuming a session interrupted mid-run no longer crashes, startup with many skills is fast again, long sessions keep their prompt cache when the context-budget reminder appears, and the per-request engine turn limit is off by default (#11240). With 748 skills, startup drops from 83.2 s to 1.4 s, fixing the slow start since 5.1.45 (#11245). In a long session that crossed the reminder at about 714k tokens of context, the request now writes 44k cache tokens instead of 758k. engineTurns.maxPerUserInput turns the turn limit back on; the 6-hour turn runtime cap still applies. Compaction finds a shorter safe tail when the summary would not fit. Kimi Code serves Kimi K3.1 Pro. A session that restarts or clears its queue no longer receives another session's queued messages twice. Claude subscription sessions run their SDK children in the session's project folder, so home-level Claude Code memory and settings no longer load into every session. MCP forms cancel instead of submitting empty answers when dismissed. Claude Code auto memory is never injected into Anthropic subscription sessions.

A ulw-research run cannot produce a reader-facing artifact against an undecided brief. The deliverable interview now also records a ## Fidelity block, and the new scripts/fidelity-policy.mjs registry turns it into the rules the CLI enforces: fact fill, fact verify, fact pairs, fact meaning-check, fact polish-apply and fact polish-diff refuse an unlocked brief with one fidelity_interview_incomplete defect and write nothing, each reading --brief (default $SESSION_DIR/brief.md), while collection keeps running; fact fill then refuses a derived row under derived numbers: none, evaluates the source floor live against each source's meta.json, binds a translation to its quote only under languages ... with translation beside, and omits a twice-failed sentence under fallback: omit. Off-table derived numbers or fallback values are usage errors instead of silently allowing; under any floor but standard a row with no source is refused; and the fill map records the brief's hash, so fact verify refuses an edition filled under a different brief (brief_changed) and re-checks every value against the current one. (#11166, closes #11009)

Fixed

  • Remove automatic process_crashed telemetry that used ordinary usage settings without separate crash consent. Starting a session no longer harvests operational crash records; manual /bug reports remain separate. (#11258) (#11280)
